- **cfn-megaplan**: Tiered planning orchestrator. Runs the full SPARC+ pipeline (research, spec, decide, pseudo, data, arch, ux, design, test, ops) as a parallel DAG, scaled by build stage (mvp/beta/enterprise) via inclusion profiles. Enforces two gates: every success criterion is executable (verifiable-done) and every step is unambiguous (haiku-executable). Plain plan mode is the default for feature work; use this only for enterprise, compliance, ops-heavy or migration-rehearsal builds (mvp/beta programs: cfn-megaplan-fast). ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan) · ★13)
- **cfn-megaplan-fast**: Token-lean planning orchestrator for multi-part programs and single features. Program mode runs spec/data/arch/ux ONCE, then only test_plan + write_plan + Bar A per part, reading section extracts not whole artifacts. Hard byte caps per artifact, static-only bars (1 round, inline patch), opus only for spec+arch, no nested subagents. Same hand-off contract to /cfn-loop-task as megaplan. Use for mvp/beta work; full /cfn-megaplan stays for enterprise/compliance/ops-heavy builds. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan-fast) · ★13)
- **cfn-megaplan-lite**: Balanced-cut planning mode for medium features (3-7 files, single shared-state surface). Runs a 7-level DAG at mvp depth but roughly half the wall-clock of full megaplan: both completion bars capped to 1 round, pseudo folded into arch, non-core phases on sonnet, and the live haiku probe dropped. Inherits megaplan's bar scripts and phase skills by relative path. Use as the balanced-cut alternative to /cfn-megaplan for medium features. ([masharratt/claude-flow-novice](https://github.com/masharratt/claude-flow-novice/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cfn-megaplan-lite) · ★13)
- **cfn-memory-management**: Prevent and detect memory leaks in Claude CLI operations through proactive monitoring, limits, and profiling. Catches the class no mechanical gate catches: correct, coherent, tests green, and nonsense for the person using it. - **cgmf**: Drive CGMF, the LANL fission-fragment de-excitation Monte Carlo code of Talou, Stetcu, Jaffke, Rising, Lovell and Kawano (Comput. Phys. Commun. 269, 108087 (2021); BSD-3-Clause). Run and analyse event-by-event simulations of prompt fission neutrons and gammas emitted by excited fission fragments, for spontaneous fission (Cf-252/254, Pu-238/240/242/244) and neutron-induced fission (U-233/234/235/238, Np-237, Pu-239/241) from thermal to 20 MeV: average and distributional neutron and gamma multiplicities, spectra, fragment mass/charge/TKE yields, and n-gamma correlations. - **cgmlst-allele-calling**: Use when you have draft/complete genome FASTA assemblies and a defined MLST schema (schema_seed) and need to determine allelic profiles, identify known/novel alleles, or scale calling to hundreds/thousands of assemblies. - **cgmlst-core-locus-selection**: Use when you have a chewBBACA allelic profile matrix (results_alleles.tsv) over draft or complete assemblies and must reduce the wgMLST schema to loci present in ≥95% (or 95-99%) of strains for core-genome typing. - **cgmlst-schema-evaluation**: Use when a chewBBACA schema (per-locus FASTA files with full allelic diversity) must be assessed locus-by-locus, e.g. to inspect allele variability before using the schema for cg/wgMLST strain identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cgmlst-schema-evaluation))
- **cgmlst-schema-locus-matching**: Use when calling alleles of query-genome CDSs against a pre-defined cg/wgMLST schema and interpreting classification tags (EXC, INF, ASM, ALM, NIPH, NIPHEM, PLOT, LNF) in the allelic profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cgmlst-schema-locus-matching))
